## Record71 — Private durable native chat recovery added before session transition (2026-10-25T00:20:37.209914+00:00)

Previous goal turn was progress: actual automatic current-user Windows connection worked against backend205688 and corrected55package was built/static verified. This turn audited session transition prerequisites and found BsChatIdeFullView's Auto-Save Active footer had no backing transcript/draft persistence: conversation history was a memory-only ten-pair list, and app close had no chat flush. Kept existing53PID324868/creation1792882867.1417208 open rather than silently dropping it. Its existing in-memory transcript has NOT been exported/imported or retroactively persisted by this source change. Full web-then-native objective stays ACTIVE/incomplete; LIVEweb52unchanged.

Added native_studio/chat_recovery.py. Default storage is under the current user's LOCALAPPDATA/AI-BS/NativeStudio/ChatRecovery with an ownerSID-derived profile directory; it uses the existing strict Windows private-folder verifier (current user/System/Administrators, protected DACL). No C-root recipient data dependency, credentials, credential enumeration, backend DB initialization, service activation or cloud transmission. All links/junctions are rejected; existing folder permissions are inspected and never rewritten. Synthetic fixtures use isolated temporary folders and real Windows permissions.

Recovery retains transcript plain text, unsent input, scratchpad, recent model-context pairs and selected model. Immutable sequence-numbered JSON snapshots include checksums, bounded size, exclusive staging, fsync, actual readback and Windows atomic no-overwrite rename. A Windows file lock protects sequence allocation across writers, and an in-process lock serializes I/O. Earlier snapshots and failed partial files are never deleted. Reader verifies schema/types/sequence/checksum; a damaged newest snapshot falls back to an earlier valid copy with a visible warning. All-invalid history is retained and reported, never silently replaced by an empty conversation. Plain-text recovery does not certify preservation of every rich hyperlink/document-format property or a complete multi-conversation browser; those remain behavioral parity work.

Integrated a GUI recovery controller: load/save work uses the existing worker bus, Send/input wait only for initial local recovery, failure restores typing with truthful status and Retry recovery. Periodic coalesced saves confirm actual worker success before saying saved; errors stop automatic retries and keep evidence. Edits made programmatically during pending recovery are kept; earlier saved copies remain intact. Restored text/context is never sent to a model, parsed as a command, or automatically executed. Close event flushes the latest current draft before worker shutdown and refuses closure on failed recovery/save or an active response. Generation fencing plus a shared save lock prevent a queued older snapshot from becoming the newest record after final-close flush. Existing native53binary is unchanged and kept open.

Tests: initial RED12missingmodule failures retained. Seven actual filesystem/permission fixtures and seven real offscreen Qt widget fixtures now pass (14new): full field round trip, restart, append-only old-byte preservation, tamper/truncation fallback/warning/all-invalid refusal, fsync failure/partial retention, bounds/schema, redirected snapshot, no command replay, load readiness, save acknowledgement/coalescing, final-close/disk failure, pending-edit preservation, unreadable-history write refusal and stale-queued-write fencing. One first widget run exposed btn_send versus the actual send_btn attribute; corrected once, then green. Combined81tests pass, retaining baseline/FE/Qt/cold-import/WAV/local-connection coverage. No actual model response, host command, financial action or production chat content was used in fixtures.

The local provider reads one supplied public HTTP(S) text page using a pinned, validated public IPv4 address and verified TLS hostname. It checks robots.txt first, refuses inaccessible policies and conservatively applies Disallow/wildcard rules even over Allow exceptions, honors crawl delay/request rate with minimum two-second per-origin spacing, and allows one concurrent reader. IPv6-only sites, redirected pages, JavaScript-only pages and bulk crawls remain unsupported. DDGS still supplies external web discovery; this is not a replacement internet-wide search index. ## Record137 — FE Express driver logs, shipment paperwork and printable load packets — 2026-10-27T02:19:44.547500+00:00
Operator requested daily driver records, weekly reports, assigned shipments/rate confirmations and printable signed delivery paperwork. Added Daily logs & weekly reports to FE Express with identical Dispatcher and DriverReports components in all four mirrors. Owner Devin and existing contractor drivers supported. Daily records capture loaded/deadhead miles, driving/other-duty hours, fuel gallons/cost, other expenses and notes. Weekly Monday-Sunday totals use weighted fuel price, flag missing log days and count agreed shipment rates once in the scheduled delivery week. Rates are not paid revenue; the expense subtotal is not profit. Manually entered hours are not a certified electronic logbook or payroll system.

Shipment assignments retain load number, driver, route, broker, agreed rate, dates, status and optional existing-haul reference. Upload/download PDF/JPEG/PNG rate confirmations, BOL, POD and receipts. UUID retry identity and optimistic revisions prevent silent duplicate/revision overwrites. Authenticated uploads reside as private BLOBs in the existing host SQLite database, with lengths/SHA256 receipts, filename/MIME guards and 10MiB/file,20files/50MiB/shipment limits. Weekly ZIP contains JSON, printable HTML, formula-escaped CSV and original paperwork under generated UUID paths, capped at200documents/64MiB input. No document enters Firebase Hosting.

Printable load packet requires explicit selection of a rate confirmation and a BOL/POD from the same shipment. It generates a separate grayscale PDF with rate confirmation first and uploaded delivery paperwork second. Original bytes remain downloadable unchanged. This is an upload/print workflow, not signature creation or independent signature verification; no automatic emailing. PyMuPDF is an existing pinned dependency. Hash checks precede rendering; unreadable/encrypted documents fail, max30pages, page dimensions checked,150dpi capped2048pixels per axis,32MiB output ceiling. Raster print copies omit active PDF actions/attachments/forms and do not preserve original cryptographic signatures. Synthetic two-page sample visually inspected for clear text and visible grayscale sample signature. Real signed shipment packet acceptance remains pending operator documents.

### Record168 - BS-Chat attachment upload defect investigation & hardening complete - 2026-10-27T20:20:00Z
Operator requested Option 2 from docs/GEMINI_HANDOFF_2026-10-27.md: investigate & fix the BS-Chat attachment upload defect (Unexpected end of JSON input), trace frontend/src/components/ChatTab.jsx (4 mirrors) and backend upload handler (/api/chat/attach in backend/AI_BS_Backend.py), write an offline request-level test in scripts/tests/, apply surgical fix, and verify 100% 4-mirror SHA-256 byte parity.
Root cause & findings:
- Frontend defect: In handleFileUpload, const data = await res.json(); was called directly without checking res.ok or verifying body presence. Empty bodies (0 bytes from network drop or timeout) threw SyntaxError: Unexpected end of JSON input. Plain text (e.g. 401 Unauthorized from Go gateway) and HTML error pages threw SyntaxError: Unexpected token ... is not valid JSON. Catch block displayed err.message directly in setErrorPopup modal. In addition, X-Client-ID: stehouwer_publishing was omitted from request headers, and no client-side fallback existed for text/code/image files when backend was offline. Identical unguarded upload calls existed in MobileGeminiChat.jsx and MobileStehouwerChat.jsx.
- Backend vulnerabilities: Endpoint chat_attach_file in backend/AI_BS_Backend.py did not sanitize filenames with os.path.basename, permitting Windows path traversal (e.g. C:\fakepath\... or relative traversal). In addition, contents.decode("utf-8") crashed on non-strict UTF-8 files with UnicodeDecodeError, and UPLOAD_ATTACHMENTS_DIR recreation was not asserted.
Surgical fixes applied:
- backend/AI_BS_Backend.py: chat_attach_file sanitizes filenames via os.path.basename(raw_filename).strip(), creates UPLOAD_ATTACHMENTS_DIR safely, implements two-tier text decoding (UTF-8 with latin-1 errors="replace" fallback), and returns safe_filename.
- frontend/src/components/ChatTab.jsx (all 4 mirrors): passes X-Client-ID header, reads response defensively via await res.text() with safe JSON parse and res.ok check, and adds zero-loss client-side FileReader fallback for text/code and image attachments when backend upload fails.
- MobileGeminiChat.jsx & MobileStehouwerChat.jsx (all 4 mirrors of each): hardened with identical defensive response reading, X-Client-ID header, and client-side FileReader fallback.
Verification & tests:
- scripts/tests/test_chat_attachment_upload.py: 15/15 automated tests passed in 0.059s covering backend text/image upload, path traversal sanitization, non-strict UTF-8 decoding, whitespace filename fallback, simulated response parsing (0-byte, plain text 401, HTML 502/504, JSON 403, JSON 200), AST checks, and 100% SHA-256 parity across all 4 mirrors of ChatTab.jsx, MobileGeminiChat.jsx, and MobileStehouwerChat.jsx.
- Regression suites: scripts/tests/test_router_mount_integrity.py (13/13 tests) and scripts/tests/test_audio_router_trace.py (10/10 tests) passed cleanly (38/38 total tests passing offline).
- Cryptographic hash verification: backend/AI_BS_Backend.py (714d095c682fa38c019b644b79e2961e3fa514fce1d0bbc8a636484d71f72e01), ChatTab.jsx 4 mirrors (4d82e0a1dc582e2b83045f2a10fdadc22d3132671fe608813efa7d66abdb88ca), MobileGeminiChat.jsx 4 mirrors (2400e53157227d5699a50c9d6893d4aee6fe4eb136b7f188741d1670ca5a88a7), MobileStehouwerChat.jsx 4 mirrors (734b5fe3d3b3eab60597d4997ba897489a3b64abf087bb829a9db62f7edfdd2c).
